## Custody note

The previous v1.29 archive opened with the older `Repository_Change_Archive Addendum — Restart Capsule v11.2 → v11.3` metadata. That addendum remains preserved below as historical content, but it is not the identity of the full archive.

Historical addenda below retain their original dates, version labels, pressure-test cautions, and active-control statements as they stood when authored. Those historical statements are not current active controls unless repeated in the current public-clean source files or current control files.

## Scope of this replacement

No arithmetic, anchors, modal states, node-classes, sign conventions, operators, slash-pairs, ranges, envelopes, Mirror protocols, Machine Guards, claim-status labels, theological claims, dependency boundaries, pressure-test results, or routed historical records are changed.

## File_02 — §0.7–§0.12 pass, finalization, pressure-test, and refresh records

```markdown
### 0.7 Arithmetic Pass scope and result

Arithmetic Pass scope:
This pass checks same-side BC spans by subtraction, same-side range spans by subtraction, civil cross-axis spans by `BC + AD − 1`, exact factorization claims, Key-of-23 whole-year conversions, SKL beginning-and-anchor checks, Flood Week boundary checks, and MT four-position matrix shifts.

The Arithmetic Pass did not pressure test the file, recompute `File_34` SKL/Berossus derivations, or resolve dependency-controlled month-state readings.

Arithmetic Pass result:
All locally executable arithmetic statements in the main body check under their stated operators. Items that remain open are dependency-control issues, not corrected arithmetic errors. The retained audit items are listed in §8.6.

### 0.8 Argument Pass scope and result

Argument Pass scope:
This pass clarifies the evidential role of each claim without changing arithmetic, anchors, modal states, node-classes, operators, sign conventions, ranges, slash-pairs, theological claims, or cross-file dependencies.

Argument-control rule:
The file proceeds in this evidential order:

| Rank | Evidence type | Function in `File_02` |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Textual datum and arithmetic fact | Establish the dates, spans, ratios, and state boundaries |
| 2 | Structural inference | Relate repeated Flood, Key-of-23, cumulative, BJ, and SKL alignments |
| 3 | Shared-tradition evidence | Support comparison between SP, BJ/Jubilees, and wider Second Temple chronological grammar |
| 4 | Typological note | Explain theological fit, especially Shem, Japheth, Flood judgment, and AD 70 |
| 5 | Providential synchronization | Classify coherence that exceeds demonstrable authorial intent |
| 6 | Dependency note | Preserve material controlled by `File_18`, `File_22`, `File_34`, `File_51a`, `File_54`, or Restart Capsule v11.12 |

Argument Pass result:
The main argument is now arranged as follows. The Chronological Matrix establishes the controlled Flood states. The Logic Engine states the regular inter-tradition harmonics. The 5290 axis and Sumerian-Christological bridge state same-side BC spans. The Judgment Harmonic states the cross-axis envelope. The Shem ±2 section explains localized versus propagated anomaly states. The BJ/Jubilees section supplies shared-tradition comparison without claiming direct dependence.

Placement decision:
The SKL Short / Long register remains in the main body because `File_02` is a Sumerian-integration file and because the local SKL state labels are needed to prevent later confusion between Sumerian A, Sumerian B, `+30`, `+2`, and `+720`. Full SKL/Berossus proof remains dependency-controlled by `File_34`.

Placement decision:
The SKL +30 / `36 BC` rail remains in the main body as a secondary rail clarification. It does not carry the primary Solar / Enochian split, but it prevents the +30 Apparent Mode from being misread as a failed 6 BC calculation.

### 0.9 Author-Decision Pass scope and result

Author-Decision Pass scope:
This pass resolves audit notes that can now be settled from controlling exemplars, prior author approvals, and explicit dependency boundaries. It does not perform Finalization, pressure testing, new arithmetic discovery, or a full dependency-file rewrite.

Author-Decision Pass result:
The Noah row in the localized MT Minimum-Regular Noah/Shem pivot table is corrected to the `File_00` controlling baseline: Noah `2843/2841 BC`, death `1893/1891 BC`. This correction is not silent; the source-preservation note is retained in §6.2. The correction aligns the Noah pair with the local 600/598 Flood-age state and with the 598 ↔ 600 gear-shift section.

The LXX Long Count `3242 BC` row remains dependency-controlled as a preserved display state. It is not used in the local `780 = 6 × 130` calculation, which remains governed by LXX Regular-Minimum `3023 BC`.

The SKL Short / Long appended `3 months and 3.5 days` datum remains `File_34`-controlled. `File_02` uses the whole-year `17980` and `24510` states for local arithmetic.

The cumulative Flood Week ranges, the Solar / Enochian split, the BJ / Jubilees `343` comparison, and the `EnvNorm` / `EnvSpread` distinction remain as approved in prior passes. No further author-decision blocker remains; the remaining dependency notes are retained as dependency controls rather than blockers.

### 0.10 Finalization Pass scope and result

Finalization Pass scope:
This pass marks the file Final, regularizes final-source status, confirms that prior passes have been incorporated, and preserves the remaining dependency controls in the audit ledger. It does not perform pressure testing, open new dependency-file derivations, recompute SKL/Berossus tables, alter arithmetic, or revise modal-state logic.

Finalization Pass result:
`File_02` is now Final. No author-decision issues remain open. LXX Long Count `3242 BC`, the SKL Short / Long appended `3 months and 3.5 days` datum, and SP/LXX 1656 endpoint relations remain dependency-controlled rather than unresolved blockers. Pressure testing was not part of the Finalization Pass; the post-final pressure-test result is recorded in §0.11 and §8.8.
```

# Repository_Change_Archive Addendum — Files 36–40 Public-Clean Cleanup and File_40 Day 2 Correction

Title: Repository_Change_Archive Addendum — Files 36–40 Public-Clean Cleanup and File_40 Day 2 Correction

## Purpose

This addendum records the public-clean cleanup of `File_36` through `File_40` and the follow-up `File_40` v1.1 correction.

The cleanup was not a substantive revision pass. It prepared already-finalized Markdown files for one stable WordPress page per file on 490d.com, with raw Markdown source retained as the canonical layer.

The follow-up `File_40` v1.1 correction was author-directed after public-clean cleanup. It removes the erroneous Day 2 named-mechanism tag `Priestly expansion`, clears the associated audit flag, and preserves the only intended Day 2 point:

```markdown
20886 − 19416 = 1470 = 3 × 490 = 7 × 210
```

No arithmetic, anchors, modal states, node-classes, sign conventions, operators, slash-pairs, ranges, envelopes, Mirror protocols, Machine Guards, theological claims, or dependency boundaries were changed. `File_40` Day 2 claim-status wording was corrected by author direction: the erroneous `source assertion; arithmetic unstated` / open-audit mechanism language was removed because the active claim is the arithmetic P19-to-SKL-Flood relation only.

Intermediate archive note:
An earlier intermediate Files 36–40 archive record preserved the File_40 Day 2 mechanism flag as open. This addendum supersedes that stale archive disposition. The active File_40 public-clean source is `File_40_Public_Clean_v1_1.md`, and the Day 2 issue is closed.

Control basis after follow-up: 490d Repository Style Guide v2.5; Restart Capsule v11.13; State Vocabulary Register v1.14; Project Procedures v3.2.

## Purpose

This addendum records two connected operations.

1. The public-clean cleanup of `File_51a`, `File_54`, and `File_55`.
2. The immediate follow-up bounded control registration that closed the `File_51a` handoff issue and the `File_55` standard SKL Creation rail-contraction registration issue.

The cleanup was not a substantive revision pass. It prepared already-finalized Markdown files for one stable WordPress page per file on 490d.com, with raw Markdown source retained as the canonical layer.

The follow-up control-registration pass was also not a substantive revision pass. It added state-vocabulary and Machine Guard retrieval controls for already-present local logic, advanced the Register to v1.14, advanced the Restart Capsule to v11.13, and refreshed the Style Guide v2.5 active companion-control pointer.

No arithmetic, anchors, active modal-state logic, node-classes, sign conventions, operators, slash-pairs, ranges, envelopes, Mirror protocols, claim-status labels, theological claims, or dependency boundaries were changed.

Raw `File_51b` was not required for this update. `File_51b` remains needed only when its own public-clean cleanup begins.

## File_51b revision sequence record

### Pass 0

Pass 0 completed the File Intake Sheet, risk rating, active-state inventory, primary-anchor inventory, major-operator inventory, dependency map, likely problem areas, section-level revision plan, audit notes, and controlling exemplars for raw `File_51b`.

No file text was revised during Pass 0.

### Pass 1

Pass 1 converted the inherited raw former `File_51a` §14 material into current repository form. It added:

- current file header and H1;
- Related Repository Files table;
- working state register;
- File map;
- Machine Guards;
- Smith LXX source-reconstruction note;
- claim-status restraint;
- audit notes;
- unresolved-issues section;
- recommended cross-file updates;
- Revision Log.

Pass 1 also normalized raw sectioning from former §14 into local `File_51b` numbering, converted tabular material to Markdown tables, and removed comma-grouped numerals such as `11111`, `11110`, `11100`, `22222`, `8000000`, and `4000000`.

No arithmetic, anchors, modal states, node-classes, sign conventions, operators, Mirror protocols, theological claims, or dependency boundaries were intentionally changed.

### Pass 2 and author-decision integration

Pass 2 verified the Pass 1 source, then integrated author decisions.

Author decisions applied:

| Issue | Final handling |
|---|---|
| LXX `5556 BC` | Resolved as actual LXX `+60 Terah` / Year-6 state, not a rounded state. The `5556 + 5556 − 1 = 11111` expression is retained as a completed Year-6 boundary expression, not a second civil-span operator. |
| `11111` | Resolved as a base-10 palindrome bridge. `File_51b` may state the local palindrome fact; `File_52a` / `File_52b` retain the inverse-number proof burden. |
| SP `4446 BC` local scaling | Pruned as non-load-bearing to avoid confusion. SP base-10 scaling section, state-register row, Machine Guard, unresolved issue, anchor-list entries, and recommended Register row removed. |
| Actual / rounded labels | Tightened through explicit node-class and state labels. |
| Proof-language calibration | Strong proof, design, irreducibility, and perfection language removed from the active argument. The file states arithmetic displays and dependency boundaries without making independent design claims. |
| Protocol 2 | Retained as midpoint-pivot logic. It is not a blanket `+1` correction to Protocol 1. |
| Biological / Levitical scaling | Clarified as dependency-controlled calendar / comparison material: `270 = 9 × 30`; `280 = 40 × 7`; male `7 + 33 = 40`; `280 + 40 = 320`; female `14 + 66 = 80`; `280 + 80 = 360`. |
| `§23.7` source reference | Resolved as a dependency reference to `File_51a`, §23.7, “The 5-Year Mod-5 Blocks.” |

### Source-reference resolution

The inherited `§23.7` reference in the Mod-5 Geometric Boundary Check was resolved as follows:

```markdown
Dependency note:
The inherited `§23.7` reference points to `File_51a`, §23.7, “The 5-Year Mod-5 Blocks.” That section controls the rounded mod-5 boundary-state block logic. `File_51b` may cite that boundary-state rule, but it does not replace `File_51a` as the controlling source for the mod-5 scaffold.
```

This closed the final unresolved source-reference issue before Finalization.

# Repository_Change_Archive Addendum — Files 46–49 Public-Clean Cleanup and File_32 / File_48 Mod-210 Resolution

File: Repository_Change_Archive_Addendum_Files_46_49_Public_Clean_File32_File48_Mod210_Resolution
Title: Repository_Change_Archive Addendum — Files 46–49 Public-Clean Cleanup and File_32 / File_48 Mod-210 Resolution
Status: Historical record; non-controlling; produced June 27 2026 during public-clean publication cleanup for `File_46` through `File_49`, followed by the bounded File_32 / File_48 mod-210 start-residue projection resolution
Canonical source: Markdown
Archive basis: `Repository_Change_Archive_Files_01_45_51a_51b_54_55_File44_Register_Update_Full_Replacement_v1_13.md` preserved exactly as prefix.
Control basis: 490d Repository Style Guide v2.5; Restart Capsule v11.14; State Vocabulary Register v1.16; Project Procedures v3.2; File_00 public-clean archive-routing precedent.

## Purpose

This addendum records two connected operations.

1. The public-clean cleanup of `File_46`, `File_47`, `File_48`, and `File_49`.
2. The author-directed minimum safe fix resolving the File_48 cross-file audit item by adding a compact File_32 start-residue projection note and replacing the File_48 unresolved audit note with a resolved convergence note.

The cleanup was not a substantive revision pass. It prepared already-revised Markdown files for one stable WordPress page per file on 490d.com, with raw Markdown retained as the canonical source layer.

The File_32 / File_48 follow-up was also not a substantive revision pass. It added a pointer-alignment note explaining that File_48's mod-210 start residues `11 / 101 / 191` are the `Q`-start projections of File_32's center corridors `15 / 105 / 195`, because `Center = Q + 4`.

## File_32 / File_48 mod-210 resolution record

### Scope

The author authorized the minimum safe fix for the File_48 mod-210 start-residue audit item.

The fix consisted of:

1. a compact File_32 clarification note under §1.6 Mod 210 corridor centers;
2. a File_48 audit-note replacement marking the item resolved.

This was a pointer-alignment and state-vocabulary closure only. It did not revise the prime-grid arithmetic, change the Modulo 210 corridor state, create a new civil-date operator, or change File_48's statistical claims.

### File_32 v1.1 compact clarification

File_32 now states:

```markdown
Start-residue projection note:
The active File_32 Modulo 210 corridor state is stated by quad centers: `15`, `105`, and `195 mod 210`. When the same corridor is viewed from the prime-quadruple start `Q`, the corresponding start residues are `11`, `101`, and `191 mod 210`, because `Center = Q + 4`.

This start-residue view is used by `File_48` for cumulative-prime gap accumulation. It is a projection of the same File_32 prime-lattice corridor state, not a redefinition of the Modulo 210 corridor and not a civil-date operator.
```

### File_48 v1.1 resolved note

File_48 now states:

```markdown
File_32 start-residue projection:
File_48's `11 / 101 / 191` mod-210 observation concerns prime-quadruple start residues used in cumulative-prime gap accumulation. File_32 states the same Modulo 210 corridor by quad centers `15 / 105 / 195`. Since `Center = Q + 4`, the two forms agree: `11 → 15`, `101 → 105`, and `191 → 195`.

This is convergence with File_32's Modulo 210 corridor state, not a new File_48 corridor definition and not a civil-date operator.

No recommended cross-file update remains from this issue.
```

## Archive-routing result

The following material is routed to the Repository_Change_Archive rather than repeated in each public file body:

- two-batch trust-status equalization records;
- Register Agreement sweep results;
- active-control pointer-refresh records;
- focused verification ledgers;
- final header spot-check ledgers;
- package / report SHA-256 records;

# Repository_Change_Archive Addendum — File_50a_50b / File_50c Post-Equalization Pressure Tests

Status: Historical record; non-controlling; appended after post-equalization sampled QA pressure tests of File_50a_50b and File_50c; active controls remain 490d Repository Style Guide v2.5, Restart Capsule v11.15, Project Procedures v3.2, and State_Vocabulary_Register v1.17.

## Summary

This addendum records the paired pressure tests of the File_50 Naturalized Grid and Bipartite Grid Landing layers after the repository-wide trust-status equalization pass.

| File | Result | Replacement |
|---|---|---|
| `File_50a_50b` | Passed pressure test; two non-argument metadata / trust-status corrections applied | `File_50a_50b_Public_Clean_v1_1_Trust_Status_Equalized_Pressure_Tested_v1_1.md` |

## File_50a_50b pressure-test note

The Naturalized Master Table was checked row by row against `P_nat = P − 11`, `V = round(P_nat / 90) × 90`, `Civil = V + 6`, and `D = P_nat − V`. All 100 rows passed. Every Original value was checked as a valid prime-quadruplet start. The P1 bypass, P2 grid-origin state, P2–P100 mod-30 / mod-210 residue rules, and verified Diff distribution `35 / 34 / 30` were retained. The File_50b P1–P24 subset was checked against the master table. No arithmetic or argument-bearing content changed.

Corrections applied: one stale audit-note sentence saying the 100-row table was not reproduced was corrected because §1.1a now carries the table; the top Revision basis was expanded to include `File_00 foundation / archive-routing precedent`.

## File_50c pressure-test note

The Bipartite Grid Landing operator, exact-strike inventory, Appendix A target matrices, target-admission rules, and hit ledger were checked against the File_50a master-table inputs. The scan reconfirmed `72` silent indices, `28` hitting indices, and `16` structural target families. All primary and source-parenthetical coordinate triples preserve exact `720` spacing; the Sumerian B / SKL Long rows preserve the stated `+6480` shift relative to Sumerian A / SKL Short through the Flood row. No target matrix, hit set, target count, arithmetic, or argument-bearing content changed.

## Control note

These pressure tests are historical / QA records. They do not alter active Style Guide, Restart Capsule, Project Procedures, State Vocabulary Register, or File_00 controls.

## File_33 pressure-test note

The File_33 test checked the File_32-dependent positive and negative prime-polarity namespace, P23 polarity-square lock, Temple Triad / Sanctuary Ennead, ordinal-extension rows, Key-of-23 Pillar arithmetic, civil cross-axis spans, exact-ratio expansions, and retained conditional probability product. All proof-bearing arithmetic passed. The only arithmetic correction was the local P48 `M` rail metadata value `166876 BC`, corrected to `166866 BC` under File_32 ordinary `M⁺ = Q + 25` for P48 `Q = 166841`.
